TITLE: Japanese national focus tree
URL: https://hoi4.paradoxwikis.com/Japanese_national_focus_tree
REVISION: 2026-03-11T09:38:51Z (rev 98519)
VERIFIED FOR: 1.17
SOURCE: WikiTeam dump wiki-hoi4.paradoxwikis.com-20260330 (archive.org)
LICENSE: CC BY-SA, https://central.paradoxwikis.com/Central:Copyrights

Japan, as one of the seven major powers, has a unique national focus tree.

Japanese national focus tree. Clicking on the branches leads to the appropriate section.

The Japanese national focus tree can be divided into 6 branches and 20 sub-branches, with 3 additional branches enabled if the DLC ncns is disabled:

The following branches are only available if the ncns DLC is not enabled:
- The Unthinkable Option Branch
- The communism branch is based around the idea of establishing a Communism Communist state in Japan with the possibility of either joining the Soviet Union or forming a faction with Chinese Soviet Republic.
- This is the hardest path in the focus tree, as it causes a civil war to erupt in the country. Even if the communists win, it will cause the entire Japanese Navy, all of Japan's colonial possessions (Korea and Mengjiang) and most of the generals and field marshals to revert to Manchukuo, which becomes independent.

- Strengthen the Civilian Government Branch
- The Democracy branch starts a civil war with the fascists and installs a Democracy . It allows Japan either join the United KingdomFrance Allies or form its own faction; the West Pacific Treaty Organisation. It also makes Japan liberate Korea and Manchukuo but, in exchange, allows Japan to puppet or annex the Philippines, the Dutch East Indies, British Malaya and Indochina.
- Similar to the Communism Communist above, this path will cause a civil war to erupt in the country and will cause Manchukuo to become independent and gain control over Korea and Mengjiang, however, Japan will retain control of both its navy and army.

- Support the Kodoha Faction Branch
- The Neutrality Monarchist Branch is focused on deposing the fascists and either empowering the Emperor of Japan or restoring the Japanese Shogunate in a modern form. Furthermore, it focuses on preparing for war with the Soviet Union and joining the German Reich's faction. This path notably militarises Manchukuo even further, by giving them Infantry Equipment and Aircraft Research Bonuses and it also creates a Non-Aggression Pact with China, however, it also requires Japan to join the London Naval Treaty, thus restricting their Ship sizes. It also accelerates economy laws, mobilises more manpower and gives a Research Slot. It also enables Kamikaze Strikes and unlocks a unique type of aircraft; the Suicide Bomb Craft, which are essentially flying human-guided missiles that deal extremely high damage, but both the pilot and the aircraft are lost in the endeavour.

The following branches are available if the ncns DLC is enabled:

- Okada's Military Purge Speech Branch
- This branch sets up a civil war in order to install a Communist or Democratic form of government by infiltrating states and swaying military officers. The player may decide to depose Emperor Hirohito and install his brother Nobuhito as Emperor Eian if Gather Royal Pacifists' Support is completed.

 - Appoint New Generals/Implement Army Reforms Sub-branch
 - These focuses are available when the civil war starts, but are unavailable if the player finishes The Red Sun Rises for a New Era focus. It modifies the Imperial Army and State Shintoism.

 - The Red Sun Rises for a New Era Sub-branch
 - For the Communist sub-branch, after the civil war, Japan may choose a leader, and depending on the leader, will then follow one of two paths. The leftmost path installs the Lecture Group, who gets their influence from Marxist-Leninist ideology. They focus on building industry in urban regions and establish a five year plan to build that industry. The rightmost path installs the Labor-Farmer Faction into power which focuses on investing in rural regions, reform the economy and unlocks the War Communism economy law.

 - Revitalize the Spirit of the Ikko Ikki Sub-branch
 - The hidden Buddhist Socialism branch is available when Japan hires both Girō Seno'o from the JAP_incorporate_buddhist_socialism focus, Jun Tsuji from the Cooperate with Anarchists focus as political advisors. Japan must also have Kanson Arahata as the country leader from the The Labor-Farmer Faction Ascendant focus. Once finishes the JAP_raise_the_red_flag_high focus, it gets an event that allows Girō Seno'o as the country's leader. Then, these hidden focuses will become available. Japan can become the Jobukkokudo or the Pure Land as well as military bonuses.

 - Socialism in One State Sub-branch
 - This path is available only if Japan selects a leader from the Lecture Group to power. This path focuses on the annexation of territory and the creation of the Fifth International to rival the Soviet Union's Comintern. It creates the Pacific Union of Soviet Socialist Republics in order to fight and defeat the Soviet Union.

 - Guide the Lesser States Sub-branch
 - This path is only available if Japan selects a leader from the Labor-Farmer Faction to power. It focuses on creating puppets from Asian and Pacific countries and integrates their economies in order to declare war against the Soviet Union, United States and the United Kingdom.

 - Organize a General Election Sub-branch
 - The Democratic path sets Japan towards a constitutional monarchy. It may decide to select the Rikken Minseitō or the Rikken Seiyūkai as the ruling political party. Japan may then select a prime minister with various different bonuses.

 - Crush the Internal Factions Sub-branch
 - This section in the Democratic path builds Japan's economic and industrial capacity.

 - Safeguard the Shanghai Settlement/Finish the Fight/Rekindle the Old Alliance Sub-branch
 - This section focuses on creating or joining Democratic factions, expansion and requesting control over Asian colonies and economic integration with faction members. It may then fully integrate puppets in order to form the Greater East Asian Federation.

- Revere the Emperor, Destroy the Traitors Branch
- This path sets Japan to follow the old ways after a coup. Emperor Hirohito may support this coup or be forced into house arrest or assassinated. His brother Yasuhito may then become Sesshō or the regent to Emperor Naruhito, or he may take the throne as Emperor Genka. The branch is then split into six paths. From left to right, these paths focus on gaining advisors, industrial bonuses, stability and political power, war against communist nations, and military bonuses including the annexation of several of Japan's puppets.

 - Embrace the Kokutai Sub-branch
 - The middle path would then either place a shogun or the Emperor into power. It will then propagandize Japan's history for bonuses and opportunities to expand and integrate other Asian states.

 - Embodying Empress Jingu/Yatagarasu's Guidance Sub-branch
 - These focuses require Empress Nagako, Emperor Hirohito's wife, as the country leader. In order to unlock these focuses, Hirohito has to be in house arrest. In order to get this, when the focus Revere the Emperor, Destroy the Traitors is complete, an event trying to convince the Emperor to support the new government will happen. Hirohito must not support the new government when selecting the "We have other candidates in mind". In order to definitely get this outcome, there must be at least 4 cavalry units in 282. Then Hirohito will need to be placed in house arrest via the next event. This will put his brother, Yasuhito, on the throne. When the player finishes the focus the Yamato Spirit. Japan will then follow an event chain that has the Emperor assassinated and then follow the chain to where Empress Nagako seeks revenge. Japan will be able to gain special project research bonuses and leader bonuses for Empress Nagako.

The following branches are available whether or not if the ncns DLC is enabled:

 - Hokushin-ron Sub-branch
 - Japan follows the policy to expand north into Siberia from Manchuria. It gives bonuses to Manchukuo, allows a temporary peace with China, and declares war against the Soviet Union for Siberia. Once complete, it gives resource bonuses in the newly conquered territory.

 - Nanshin-ron Sub-branch
 - Japan follows the policy to expand south into Indochina, Indonesia and then into Australia and New Zealand. It allows a temporary peace with the Soviet Union. Once complete, it gives resource bonuses in the newly conquered territory.

 - Revisit the Twenty-One Demands Sub-branch
 - In this section, Japan expands into the Asian mainland. The leftmost section puppets Mengjiang and expands into Mongolia and Inner Mongolian territory. The rightmost section expands into China, launch offenses into Chinese states and creates the Reorganized National Government of the Republic of China. It also pushes the country farther into Asia, either declaring war on or inviting Tibet, Bhutan, Nepal and Afghanistan to the faction.

- Purge the Kodoha Faction Branch
- The historical path starts by choosing to support the civilian government which gives Japan to temporarily ally China against the Communists or the military which makes an expansionist faction. It will then be able to build up State Shintoism and
 Showa Statism. Japan may then propose the Tripartite Pact with the Fascist majors of Europe.

 - The Imperial Rule Assistance Association Sub-branch
 - Japan will then become a Fascist nation. It may then choose either to have Hideki Tōjō from the Army, Isoroku Yamamoto from the Navy as Prime Minister, or have the country directly ruled by Emperor Showa. Japan may build up the Greater East Asia Co-Prosperity Sphere and integrate the members' economies. Japan can then become the Empire of Great Japan and core various Pacific Islands.

 - Solidify Tohokai Rule Sub-branch
 - This hidden focus is only available when Japan takes the Empower the Thought Police focus and the Civic Faction is at least influential. Then when it takes the JAP_imperial_rule_assistance_association focus, Japan gets an event where the Thought Police demands Seigō Nakano as the Prime Minister. Choosing their candidate will make this focus available.

- The Koiso Cabinet Branch
- This hidden branch deals with Japan losing their offensive war and the chaos associated with it. It is available via an event with specific requirements. Japan must: exist, has not capitulated, is not in a civil war, is fully independent, is in an offensive war, and the current country leader must either be Hideki Tōjō, Isoroku Yamamoto or Seigō Nakano. Japan must have either lost 526, 645, 646, 524, or 555 or if Japan is nuked. It replaces the leader with Kuniaki Koiso. Next, sets off The Kyūjō Incident event which either makes Japan surrender in accordance to the will of the Emperor and accept the Potsdam Declaration and keep the Home Islands, or continue the war until the end with bonuses defending in core territory. Naruhiko Higashikuni or Prince Yasuhiko Asaka, members of the Imperial Family will then become the country's leader. If Japan accepts the Potsdam Declaration, Tetsu Katayama will become the Prime Minister under a Democratic government.

- Ministry of Commerce and Industry Branch
- The economy branch builds Japan's industrial capacity and upgrades the dynamic modifier Early Industrialization Efforts or The Economic Miracle. It gives influence to the Zaibatsu Faction if Japan decides to approach the big four Zaibatsu: Mitsui, Mitsubishi, Sumitomo or Yasuda for support. Those focus' time requirement may be cut in half if the player takes either Guide the Zaibatsus or Work With the Zaibatsus focuses.

 - Ministry of Colonial Affairs Sub-branch
 - This section gives Japan's colonies: Manchukuo, Taiwan, its Pacific islands and Korea industrial bonuses.

- The Imperial Defense Plan Branch
- The military branch of the focus tree. It starts with bonuses when Japan declares war against a neighboring country or a country with capital ships.

 - Expand Kawagoe Army Arsenal Sub-branch
 - This section of the tree modifies the Imperial Army, gives bonuses to army technology as well as increase the Army Faction 's influence.

 - Establish Showa Aircraft Industry Sub-branch
 - This section modifies the Army and Naval Air Services gives bonuses to air force technologies and then consolidates research and creates the Japanese Air Force.

 - Expand Hiratsuka Navy Ammunitions Arsenal Sub-branch
 - This section of the tree modifies the Imperial Navy, gives bonuses to naval technology as well as increase the Naval Faction 's influence.

 - Combined Nuclear Research Efforts Sub-branch
 - The final two focuses of the military branch gives nuclear research bonuses as well as end the imperial influence between the Army and Navy.

# The Unthinkable Option Branch [verified for 1.17]

 ncns Available only with the No Compromise, No Surrender DLC disabled.

Communist branch of the Japanese national focus tree.
Clicking on a national focus icon leads to the appropriate table row.

# Strengthen the Civilian Government Branch [verified for 1.17]

 ncns Available only with the No Compromise, No Surrender DLC disabled.

Democratic branch of the Japanese national focus tree.
Clicking on a national focus icon leads to the appropriate table row.

## Embrace the Kokutai Sub-branch [verified for 1.17]

ncns

Mythological propagandization sub-branch of the Japanese national focus tree.
Clicking on a national focus icon leads to the appropriate table row.

{| class="mildtable"
! style="width: 30%;" | Focus
! style="width: 30%;" | Prerequisites

# The Imperial Defense Plan Branch [verified for 1.17]

Military branch of the Japanese national focus tree.
Clicking on a national focus icon leads to the appropriate table row.

{| class="mildtable"
! style="width: 30%;" | Focus
! style="width: 30%;" | Prerequisites
! style="width: 40%;" | Effects

|- id = "The Imperial Defense Plan"
| FOCUS The Imperial Defense Plan: In order to protect the state, we must formulate a new plan for proactive defense. All wars will be won by sudden large-scale decisive strikes, avoiding prolonged wars and forcing our enemies to give in to our demands.
Alternative Names

| Japan:
- Is fully independent
- One of the following must be true:
 - Is a subject of another country
 - Has completed focus Implement Army Reforms
| Japan:
- When Japan declares war on a neighboring country, or a neighboring country of a subject, gain for 90 days:

Spirit First Strike Principle: The revised National Defense Policy adopted in 1936 has outlined a doctrine in which we set first-strike principal and short-term operation in a military contingency as the military modus operandi. Preparation for military force in peace time has a such been necessary for the short-term operation emphasized with consideration of long-drawn-out and all-out war. We will win this war quickly, for otherwise we may not win it at all!
- Attack bonus against country: +10.0%
- Breakthrough bonus against country: +10.0%

- When Japan declares war on a country with Capital Ships, gain for 180 days:

Spirit Kantai Kessen: With the decisive defeat of the Russian navy at the Battle of Tsushima, the navy now fully believes in a doctrine where it could would win a war by fighting and winning a single, decisive naval action. Their current plans to repeat such a victory now involve assuming a defensive posture and waiting upon the enemy fleet to approach, then destroying it in a sharp battle off the Japanese mainland.
- Capital Ship Attack Against Country: +10.0%
- Spotting Speed Against Country: +10.0%

|}

### Combined Nuclear Research Efforts Sub-branch [verified for 1.17]

End of the Military sub-branch of the Japanese national focus tree.
Clicking on a national focus icon leads to the appropriate table row.
